[1 May 793, 08:41, at Dorestat] picture pt_046_the_first_order
[1 May 793, 08:41, at Dorestat] the first order: An order at Traiectum / 2 casks of Rhenish wine for Traiectum by 11 May 793, at 46 d each, 91 d in all. / Each part is paid as it lands, less the toll there: 10 in the hundred today. Bring all of it by the day and they will think the better of you. Bring none and they will remember it; bring part and they will remember that a little. / Unanswered, the offer stands 7 days and then goes to another. ...
